Implementation details:

`evaluate_internal(a)`:
```
def convolve_auto(a): return np.convolve(a,a)
def score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return np.inf
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01: return np.inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    m=float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```
This matches evaluate_sequence without clipping if valid. To be safe, final passed to evaluate_sequence? We can call evaluate_sequence(best.tolist()) a few times to verify and maybe use as official best_score. But internal score identical for valid nonneg within 1000. We can use internal for speed. At end, call evaluate_sequence to ensure finite, if not fallback.

```
v=np.empty(n); v[0]=1; for i in range(n-1): v[i+1]=v[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2)
```
Wait factor for i from 0: v1=v0*(1)/(2)=0.5; formula (2*i+1)/(2*i+2). Good.

def two_block(n): v=np.ones(n); half=n//2; v[half:]=0.5; return v
```

`active_gradient_matrix(a, eps=1e-5)`:
```
b=np.convolve(a,a)
maxb=b.max()
if maxb<=0: return None, b
a_scaled = a / sqrt(maxb)
b_scaled = b / maxb
active = np.where(b_scaled >= 1 - 1e-6)[0]
# maybe limit active to at most max_active= n? okay
# Build G m x n
G=np.zeros((len(active),n))
for idx,k in enumerate(active):
    l=max(0, k - a.size + 1)
    r=min(a.size-1, k)
    if l<=r:
        # derivative wrt p_i = 2 * a_scaled[k-i]
        G[idx, l:r+1] = 2.0 * a_scaled[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]
return a_scaled, G
```

LP direction function:
```
def lp_direction(a_scaled, G):
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    n=a_scaled.size
    m=G.shape[0]
    if m==0: return ones? 
    cvec = np.empty(n+1); cvec[:n]=-1; cvec[n]=0.5
    A_ub = np.zeros((m,n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=G; A_ub[:,n]=-1
    b_ub = np.zeros(m)
    lows = -a_scaled.copy()
    # if some zero, allow small positive upper
    ups = np.maximum(a_scaled, 0.1) * 1.5
    # cap large? ups = np.minimum(ups, 1.0?) Since values normalized max conv1, p0 could large? Half-B p0=1. To allow increases, up=max(a,1) maybe.
    ups = np.maximum(a_scaled, 1.0)  # if p_i<1 allow up to1, if >1 allow proportional
    bounds=list(zip(lows, ups)) + [(None,None)]
    res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-7})
    if res.success:
       obj = float(cvec @ res.x) # minimize; improvement = -obj
       if obj < -1e-7:
           return res.x[:n]
    return None
```
Since cvec includes M. If active constraints none, can increase all coords; direction ones.
The LP objective homogeneous; with bounds, if improvement possible, d will hit bounds. Good.
We may need to include all constraints not just active to prevent large violation; first-order only active. But line search may violate inactive too; evaluate exact. Could add all constraints with margin: G_all (2n-1 x n) maybe too large. For n≤512, 1023x512 ~524k, okay. Use all constraints with slack? To get robust direction, use all constraints with c_k: require M ≥ ∇c_k d + α(1-c_k)? If M is max predicted c. For inactive c<1, we can allow increase up to slack: ∇c_k d - M? The actual after small step c_k ≈ c_k + t∇. To have max ≤1 + M/t? Hard. Use active only.

def line_search(a, d, current_score):
    n=len(a)
    t_max=1.0
    neg=d<0
    if any(neg): t_max=min(t_max, -np.min(a[neg]/d[neg]) - 1e-12)
    if t_max <=0: t_max=1e-3
    candidates = [t_max, 0.5*t_max, 0.25*t_max, 0.1*t_max, 0.05*t_max]
    # also maybe t=1 even if some negative clip? But if negative, clip but not linear; try clip
    for t in candidates:
       cand=a + t*d; cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); s=cand.sum(); if s<1e-6: continue
       sc=score_array(cand)
       track best
    # also try with normalization? Score scale invariant so not needed, but if maxc smaller, scaling up sum improves objective? Wait score scale invariant, so no. However if candidate has maxc<1 but sum smaller? Scaling doesn't change score. So fine.
```

def lp_search(a, time_limit):

def random_search(a, sc, time_limit, seed):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   while time...:
      # choose type
      r=rng.random()
      if r<0.3: d=rng.normal(0,1,n)
      # ensure positive sum: if d.sum()<0: d = -d
      # maybe add 1 (increase all) to leverage scaling? Since score scale invariant, adding constant can change shape.
      if rng.random()<0.3: d += rng.uniform(0.1,0.5)
      # bound d to [-a*0.5, max(a,1)*0.5]
      lo=np.maximum(-a*0.5, -0.5)
      hi=np.maximum(a*0.5, 0.5)
      d=np.clip(d, lo, hi)
      line search
   return...
```

Block perturbation for large n:
```
def block_search(a, sc, time_limit, rng):
   while time:
      width=rng.integers(max(1,n//256), max(2,n//16))
      start=rng.integers(0,n-width)
      cand=a.copy()
      mode=rng.integers(0,3)
      if mode==0: cand[start:start+width]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25))
      elif mode==1: cand[start:start+width]=a[start:start+width]*rng.uniform(0.25,2)
      else: # swap with random block? 
      cand=np.clip(cand,0,None)
      sc2=score_array(cand)
      if sc2<sc: accept
```
This is O(n log n) each for n up to 2048; can do many. But if half-B local max, not improve.

Let's outline final `propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs)`:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s)-5
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    candidates=[]
    # lengths to try
    lengths = [16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048]
    # maybe include height_sequence_1 length if <=4096
    try:
       h = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, float)
       if h.size>0: candidates.append(h)
    except: pass
    for n in lengths:
       candidates.append(half_binomial(n))
       candidates.append(two_block(n))
       # maybe half with p0 not1? skip
    # Evaluate unique and store top
    best_a, best_score = None, inf
    for a in candidates:
       a=np.clip(np.asarray(a,float),0,1000)
       if a.size==0: continue
       sc=score_array(a)
       if sc<best_score: best=...
       print
    # local search on best up to a few top candidates
    top_candidates = sorted list by score keep first 5
    for a,sc in top_candidates:
       if time > deadline-60: break
       if a.size <= 512:
          a,sc = lp_local_search(a, sc, deadline_local)
       a,sc = random_block_search(a, sc, short time)
       update best
    # maybe if best_score still >2? fallback two_block
    # ensure final length reasonable and valid
    best_a = np.clip(best_a,0,1000)
    # optionally scale so max <=1? not needed; maybe scale to sum = sqrt(2n) to keep numbers moderate
    if best_a.size>0:
        s=best_a.sum()
        if s>0: best_a = best_a * np.sqrt(2*best_a.size)/s  # align normalization, values maybe <=? half-B S~1.1√n, scale ~1.3, values ~1.3; ok.
    # validate with evaluate_sequence if possible
    try:
       official=evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist())
       if np.isfinite(official): best_score=official
    except: pass
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): best_a=np.ones(256)
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```

Simulated annealing schedule:
```
def anneal_search(a, sc, time_budget, rng, T0=0.1, Tend=1e-6):
   temp=T0
   while time:
      cand=block_perturb(a,rng)
      csc=score_array(cand)
      if csc < sc or rng.random() < np.exp((sc-csc)/temp): accept
      temp *= 0.999...
      track best
```

Functions:
```
def modulated_half(n, alpha, beta):
   h=half_binomial(n)
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   v=h*np.exp(alpha*x + beta*x*x)
   return v
```

Implementation of `lp_local_search`:
```
def lp_local_search(a, start_score, time_limit, seed=0):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    # normalize max=1 maybe
    b=np.convolve(a,a); m=b.max()
    if m>0: a=a/np.sqrt(m)
    sc=score_array(a)
    timeout deadline = time.monotonic()+time_limit
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        active_grad, active_count = build_active(a, tol=1e-6)
        d=None
        if active_grad is not None:
            d = solve_lp_direction(a, active_grad)
        if d is None and active_count < 50: # maybe try tol 1e-4
            active_grad,... tol=1e-4; d=...
        if d is None:
            # try add all constraints? no. break
            break
        # line search
        best_candidate=None; best_sc=sc
        tmax=1.0
        neg=d<0
        if np.any(neg):
            tmax=min(tmax, np.min(-a[neg]/d[neg]) - 1e-12)
        if tmax <=0: tmax=1e-3
        for t in (tmax, 0.5*tmax, 0.25*tmax, 0.125*tmax, 0.75*tmax):
            cand=a+t*d; cand=np.maximum(cand,0)
            if cand.sum() < 1e-8: continue
            cand_sc=score_array(cand)
            if cand_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=cand_sc; best_candidate=cand
        if best_candidate is not None and best_sc < sc - 1e-10:
            a=best_candidate; sc=best_sc; no_improve=0; maybe print
        else:
            no_improve+=1
            if no_improve>2: break
    return a,sc
```

Build active:
```
def build_active(a, tol=1e-6):
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    m=b.max()
    if m <=0: return None,0
    b=b/m
    active=np.where(b >= 1 - tol)[0]
    if len(active)==0: active=np.argmax(b).reshape(1)
    # cap
    max_rows=600
    if len(active)>max_rows:
       active=active[np.argsort(-b[active])[:max_rows]]
    G=np.zeros((len(active), a.size))
    for idx,k in enumerate(active):
       l=max(0,k-a.size+1); r=min(a.size-1,k)
       if l<=r:
          G[idx,l:r+1] = 2.0*a[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]
    return G,len(active)
```
Note after normalize m=1, but if not normalized, use a_scaled? If b normalized but a not, derivative should use a original? We are solving on current a (not scaled). Constraint c_k≤? We can normalize a to max=1 before LP to keep values O(1). Let's set a_norm = a / sqrt(m) and use its b normalized. Then derivative uses a_norm.

LP solve maybe fails due to unbounded M? Constraints M ≥ Gd. If G rows all zero? no. Bounds on d prevent unbounded. M unbounded below but constrained to ≥ forms; okay. Linprog may have issues with free M. Use variable M = M_pos - M_neg with both ≥0 to avoid free bounds? Simpler: bounds [(None,None)] allowed. HiGHS can.

Random block search:
Need avoid too many expensive conv for large n. Limit iterations based on n:
```
def block_search(a, sc, time_limit, rng):
   n=a.size
   deadline=...
   max_iters = 200 if n>2000 else 2000
   it=0
   while time<deadline and it<max_iters:
      it+=1
      cand=a.copy()
      width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//10)))
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
      mode=rng.integers(0,4)
      if mode==0:
         cand[start:stop]*=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25)))
      elif mode==1:
         cand[start:stop]=a[start:stop]*float(rng.uniform(0.25,2.0))
      elif mode==2:
         # set block to constant based on neighbors
         val=float(np.mean(a[start:stop]))*float(rng.uniform(0.5,2.0))
         cand[start:stop]=val
      else:
         # swap with another block
         width2=width; start2=...; cand[start:start+width], cand[start2:start2+width]=...
      cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000)
      sc2=score_array(cand)
      if sc2 < sc - 1e-12: accept
   return
```

Implementation `stateoftart_direction(f)`:
- n=f.size; M=1 after normalization (max c(f,f)=1).
- A_ub: (2n-1,n). For k in 0..2n-2, for j in 0..n-1: if 0≤k-j<n? Wait conv(f,g)[k] = ∑_{i} f[i] g[k-i]. If variable g index j, contribution when k-i=j => i=k-j. Coefficient f[k-j] if 0≤k-j<n. So row k, col j = f[k-j] if valid. We can build via slices:
   for k: l=max(0,k-n+1), r=min(n-1,k); j range l..r corresponds g[j]; coefficient f[k-j], as j increases k-j decreases. Assign A[k,l:r+1] = f[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]. (Same shape as derivative without factor 2.)
- b_ub = ones(2n-1)
- bounds [(0,None)]*n.
- c = -ones(n)
- res=linprog(c, A_ub, b_ub, bounds, method='highs')
- If success and sum(g) > sum(f)+eps, direction d = g - f (or just g). Update f_new = (1-t) f + t g, then evaluate exact line search over t.
This LP may be more expensive but solves once per iteration. Since constraints full, no active selection. It may find g exactly f at optimum if f is extreme? For half-B, maybe g=f unique? Let's test K=2 f=[1,.5]. Constraints row conv(f,g)≤1: k0 1*g0≤1 =>g0≤1; k1 g0+.5g1≤1; k2 .5g0+g1? convolution f*g length3: [g0, g0+0.5g1, 0.5g0+? wait f=[1,.5], g=[g0,g1]: c0=1*g0, c1=1*g1+.5*g0, c2=.5*g1. Constraints: g0≤1, .5g0+g1≤1, .5g1≤1. Max g0+g1. f sum1.5 feasible. Solve: g0=1, then g1≤.5, sum1.5 unique. So no move. K=3 f=[1,.5,.375]. Constraints maybe unique? likely f extreme optimum.

Line search directions: We can enhance `line_search_improve` to accept a list of directions.

```
def line_search_multi(a, directions, current_score):
   best_sc=current_score; best_cand=None
   for d in directions:
       cand,sc = line_search_improve(a,d,current_score)
       if cand is not None:
          if best_cand is None or sc<best_sc: update
   return best_cand,best_sc
```

Random direction search: We might include after LP. Use `rng` seed. For n up to 256, 5000 iterations.

```
def random_direction_search(a, start_score, time_limit, rng):
   n=a.size
   deadline=...
   max_iters=20000 if n<=128 else 5000 if n<=512 else 500
   it=0
   while ...:
      it+=1
      # create direction with positive mean
      d = rng.normal(0,1,n)
      # add uniform component to increase total
      if rng.random()<0.5:
         d += 0.1
      # bound relative to current
      lo = -0.5*a
      hi = np.maximum(0.5*a, 0.5)
      d = np.clip(d, lo, hi)
      if d.sum() <=0: d = -d; d = np.clip(d,lo,hi)
      cand,sc = line_search_improve(a,d,start_score)
      if cand is not None:
          a=cand; start_score=sc
   return a,start_score
```
This may be expensive but fine.

def make_base_candidates(seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands=[]
    lengths=[16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096]
    for n in lengths:
       cands.append(half_binomial(n))
       cands.append(two_block(n))
    # half variants n<=256
    for n in [64,128,256]:
       h=half_binomial(n)
       for r in (0.9,1.1): cands.append(np.power(h,r))
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       for al in (-0.5,0.5):
          for be in (-1.0,1.0):
             cands.append(h*np.exp(al*x+be*x*x))
    # random few
    for n in [64,128,256]:
       for _ in range(3):
          v=rng.exponential(scale=1.0,size=n)
          v=np.maximum(v,1e-6)
          cands.append(v)
    # height_sequence_1
    h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    if h is not None:
       arr=_clip_valid(h)
       if 1 <= arr.size <= 8192:
          cands.append(arr)
       elif arr.size>8192:
          # downsample by block averaging? Could produce all ones; skip or average to 4096
          arr2=np.resize(arr, 4096)? np.resize repeats not average. Use reshape blocks.
    return cands
```

Search phases in propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(0,float(budget_s)-5.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands=make_base_candidates(seed)
    top=evaluate_top(cands, 8)
    if not top: return [1.0]*64
    best_a, best_sc = top[0]
    # best searchable = top candidate with size<=512
    search_queue=[]
    for sc,a in top:
       if a.size <= 512 and len(search_queue)<3:
           search_queue.append((a.copy(), sc))
    # LP/stateofart on searchable
    for idx,(a,sc) in enumerate(search_queue):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120: break
       time_limit = min(120.0, max(10.0, (deadline - time.monotonic())/3.0))
       # stateofart first
       a2,sc2 = stateoftart_lp_improve(a, sc, time_limit*0.5)
       if sc2 < best_sc: update
       a2,sc2 = lp_active_direction_search(a2, sc2, time_limit*0.5)
       if sc2 < best_sc: update
    # block/random on global best and maybe top searchable
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 30:
       a2,sc2 = block_local_search(best_a.copy(), best_sc, min(remaining*0.5, 300), rng)
       update
    if remaining > 30:
       # random direction on best if size<=512
       a2,sc2 = random_direction_search(best_a.copy(), best_sc, min(remaining*0.3, 120), rng)
       update
    if remaining > 30:
       # block on a different top candidate
       for sc,a in top[1:]:
          if a.size <= 2048:
             a2,sc2=block_local_search(a.copy(),sc, 60, rng)
             update
    # finalize scale
    best_a=_clip_valid(best_a)
    if best_a.size>0:
       s=best_a.sum()
       if s>0:
          # scale to sum sqrt(2n) maybe, but can increase max; score scale invariant. Keep within [0,1000].
          target_sum=np.sqrt(2.0*best_a.size)
          best_a *= target_sum / s
       best_a=np.clip(best_a,0.0,1000.0)
    # ensure official score finite
    try:
       official=evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist())
    except Exception:
       official=np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(official):
       best_a=half_binomial(512)
       official=evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist())
    print("final length", best_a.size, "score", official)
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
Potential issue: scaling to sum sqrt(2n) changes values but not score; okay. However if values become >1000? half_B p0=1, target_sum/ S ≈ (1.414√n)/(1.128√n)=1.255, p0 ~1.25 <<1000. Fine.

`stateoftart_lp_improve` and `lp_active_direction_search` functions.

Implement `stateoftart_direction(f)`:
```
def stateoftart_direction(f):
    if linprog is None: return None
    n=f.size
    if n > 384: return None
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       l=max(0,k-n+1); r=min(n-1,k)
       if l<=r:
          A[k,l:r+1] = f[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]
    c=-np.ones(n)
    b=np.ones(2*n-1)
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
    if res.success and np.sum(res.x) > np.sum(f)+1e-8:
        return res.x
    return None
```
This LP may be unbounded? Constraints include g0≤f0? Since f≥0, if some f_i=0, constraints may not bound g_i? Example f with zeros at ends? f0>0 usually, c0 f0 g0≤1 bounds g0. If f has zeros, maybe some g unbounded? But A may have zero row? If f all zeros invalid. If f0=0 and f1=0..., some constraints late may bound. To be safe, add upper bounds e.g. max(f,1)*10. Use bounds [(0.0, max(1.0, np.sum(f)))]? Since objective positive, without finite bounds could be unbounded if some variable doesn't appear in any constraint? Every g_j appears in constraints k where i=k-j valid; if f_i=0 for all such i, no bound. But f likely positive; still add upper bound U = max(1.0, 2.0*np.sum(f)). Use `ups = np.full(n, U)`, `bounds=list(zip(np.zeros(n), ups))`. This makes LP bounded. U large enough. Objective max sum g ≤ nU. Good.

`line_search_improve` should be robust if d all zero: return None.
```
def line_search_improve(a, d, current_score):
    d=np.asarray(d,float)
    if d.shape != a.shape: return None, np.inf
    if not np.any(d): return None, np.inf
    tmax=1.0
    neg=d<0
    if np.any(neg):
        tav = -a[neg]/d[neg]
        tmax = min(tmax, float(np.min(tav)) - 1e-12)
    if tmax <= 1e-12: return None, np.inf
    candidates = []
    for t in (tmax, 0.9*tmax, 0.5*tmax, 0.25*tmax, 0.1*tmax, 0.05*tmax):
       if t>0 and t<=1.0: candidates.append(t)
       elif t>0: candidates.append(1.0) # allow >1? If no negatives, d bounded but can go >1; however line search tmax>1 if no neg. We may allow t up to 2 if no negatives.
```

```

`block_local_search` details:
```
def block_local_search(a, start_score, time_limit, rng):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); sc=start_score
   n=a.size
   deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
   max_iters = 5000 if n<=256 else 1000 if n<=1024 else 150
   sigma=0.2
   accepted=0
   it=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline and it<max_iters:
      it+=1
      cand=a.copy()
      minw=max(1, n//500)
      maxw=max(minw+1, n//16)
      w=int(rng.integers(minw, maxw+1))
      s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
      mode=int(rng.integers(0,4))
      if mode==0:
         cand[s:s+w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
      elif mode==1:
         cand[s:s+w] *= float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.0))
      elif mode==2:
         # local mirror
         cand[s:s+w] = np.maximum(cand[s:s+w], 0.5*np.mean(a))? no.
      else:
         # swap with another
         s2=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
         if s2<=s: s2=s+w
         cand[s:s+w], cand[s2:s2+w] = cand[s2:s2+w].copy(), cand[s:s+w].copy()
      cand=_clip_valid(cand, 1000)
      if cand.sum() < 1e-8: continue
      sc2=score_array(cand)
      if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-10:
          a=cand; sc=sc2; accepted+=1
   return a,sc
```
Swap mode could break good shape; maybe not. Use no swap or rare. Could use block copy scaling more.

Define `normalize_max(a)`:
```
def normalize_to_peak_one(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return a
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    m=float(np.max(b))
    if m>0 and np.isfinite(m):
       a=a/np.sqrt(m)
    return a
```

def random_modulation_search(base, rng, deadline):
   while time < deadline:
      n = int(rng.choice([64,128,256,512]))
      h=half_binomial(n)
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      alpha=float(rng.normal(0,0.5)); beta=float(rng.normal(0,0.5)); gamma=float(rng.normal(1,0.1)) # power
      v=h**gamma * np.exp(alpha*x+beta*x*x)
      sc=score_array(v)
      if sc < best_sc + 0.05: # near or better
          a2,sc2=block_local_search(v,sc,10,rng)
          update
```

def modulation_search(base_a, base_score, rng, deadline):
    n_choices=[64,128,256,512]
    count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and count < 2000:
       count +=1
       n=int(rng.choice(n_choices))
       h=half_binomial(n)
       x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       r=float(rng.uniform(0.85,1.15))
       al=float(rng.normal(0.0,0.6))
       be=float(rng.normal(0.0,0.6))
       v=np.power(h,r) * np.exp(al*x + be*x*x)
       v=_clip_valid(v)
       sc=score_array(v)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < base_score + 0.02:
           # short hill climb
           v2,sc2 = block_local_search(v, sc, 2.0, rng)
           if sc2 < base_score:
               base_a=v2; base_score=sc2
               print(...)
    return base_a, base_score
```

Detailed code for `score_array`:
```
def score_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0: return float('inf')
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return float('inf')
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s < 0.01: return float('inf')
    b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
    if b.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): return float('inf')
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0: return float('inf')
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))
```

    return np.clip(arr, 0.0, limit)

def line_search_improve(a, d, current_score):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); d=np.asarray(d,float)
    if a.shape != d.shape or a.size==0: return None, float('inf')
    if not np.any(d): return None, float('inf')
    tmax=2.0
    neg = d < 0.0
    if np.any(neg):
        ratios = -a[neg] / d[neg]
        tmax = float(np.min(ratios)) * (1.0 - 1e-10)
        if tmax > 1.0: tmax = 1.0
    if not np.isfinite(tmax) or tmax <= 1e-12:
        return None, float('inf')
    best_sc = current_score
    best_cand = None
    ts = [tmax, 0.75*tmax, 0.5*tmax, 0.25*tmax, 0.125*tmax, 0.0625*tmax]
    for t in ts:
        if not np.isfinite(t) or t <=0 or t > tmax: continue
        cand = a + t*d
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(cand))
        if s < 0.01: continue
        sc = score_array(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-11:
            best_sc = sc; best_cand = cand
    return best_cand, best_sc
```

```
def build_active(a, tol=1e-5, max_rows=600):
    b = np.convolve(a,a)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if m <=0 or not finite: return None
    b = b/m
    active = np.where(b >= 1.0 - tol)[0]
    if active.size ==0: active = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    if active.size > max_rows:
       ord = np.argsort(-b[active])
       active = active[ord[:max_rows]]
    G = np.zeros((active.size, a.size))
    for idx,k in enumerate(active):
       l=m... ; r=...
       if l<=r:
          G[idx,l:r+1] = 2.0 * a[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]
    return G
```
`np.argsort(-b[active])` returns order; no lambda.

`solve_active_direction`:
```
def solve_active_direction(a, G):
   if linprog is None or G is None or G.size==0: return None
   n=a.size; m=G.shape[0]
   cvec=np.empty(n+1); cvec[:n]=-1.0; cvec[n]=0.5
   A_ub=np.zeros((m,n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=G; A_ub[:,n]=-1.0
   b_ub=np.zeros(m)
   lows=-a
   ups=np.maximum(a,1.0)
   bounds=[]
   for i in range(n): bounds.append((float(lows[i]), float(ups[i])))
   bounds.append((None,None))
   try: res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
   except: return None
   if res.success:
      # cvec dot x is obj; if < -1e-7
      if float(cvec @ res.x) < -1e-7:
          return res.x[:n]
   return None
```
`cvec @ res.x` works for 1D.

def active_improve(a,start_score,time_limit):
   a = normalize_peak(np.array(a,float).copy())
   sc=score_array(a)
   deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
   no_improve=0
   it=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline and it<50 and no_improve<2:
      it+=1
      G=build_active(a, tol=1e-5, max_rows=600)
      d=solve_active_direction(a,G)
      if d is None:
         G=build_active(a, tol=1e-3, max_rows=600)
         d=solve_active_direction(a,G)
      if d is None: break
      cand,sc2=line_search_improve(a,d,sc)
      if cand is not None:
         a=cand; sc=sc2; no_improve=0
      else:
         no_improve+=1
   return a,sc
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t0+max(0.0,float(budget_s)-5.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    candidates=build_candidates(seed)
    top=evaluate_top(candidates, 12)
    if not top:
        arr=half_binomial(256); return arr.tolist()
    best_a, best_sc = top[0]
    print("initial best score ...")
    # search top candidates with LP
    for idx in range(min(4,len(top))):
        sc,a = top[idx]
        if sc > best_sc + 0.5: continue
        if a.size > 384: continue
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 60: break
        limit = min(45.0, rem/3.0)
        if limit < 5: continue
        a2,sc2 = stateoftart_improve(a, sc, limit)
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a, best_sc = a2, sc2
            print(...)
        a2,sc2 = active_improve(a2, sc2, min(30.0, max(10.0, rem/3.0)))
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a, best_sc = a2, sc2
            print(...)
    # block search best
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30:
        limit = min(120.0, rem*0.35)
        best_a,best_sc=block_local_search(best_a,best_sc,limit,rng)
        print
    # random direction on best if small
    rem=...
    if rem>30 and best_a.size <=512:
       best_a,best_sc=random_direction_search(best_a,best_sc,min(90, rem*0.3), rng)
    # modulation search
    rem=...
    if rem>60:
       best_a,best_sc=modulation_search(best_a,best_sc,rng, deadline-60)
    # small block on other top
    rem=...
    for idx in range(1,len(top)):
       if rem<60: break
       sc,a=top[idx]
       if a.size <= 2048 and sc < best_sc + 0.2:
           a2,sc2=block_local_search(a,sc,30.0,rng)
           if sc2 < best_sc: update
           rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    # final scale and validate
    best_a=clip_valid(best_a)
    if best_a.size>0:
       s=best_a.sum()
       if s>0:
          best_a = best_a * (np.sqrt(2.0*best_a.size) / s)
       best_a=clip_valid(best_a)
    try:
       official=evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist())
    except Exception:
       official=float('inf')
    if not np.isfinite(official):
       best_a=half_binomial(512)
       try: official=evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist())
       except: official=2.0
    print("final score",official)
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative step-height sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))**2.

Main ideas in this program:
1. Use truncations of (1-z)^(-1/2), i.e.
   1, 1/2, 3/8, 5/16, ...
   They give a strong scalable construction with score close to pi/2.
2. Use several related deterministic perturbations and random starts.
3. Polish candidates with:
   - a convex LP direction from the state-of-the-art update
     (maximize sum(g) subject to f*g <= M);
   - an active-constraint linearized direction search;
   - günstig block hill-climbing;
   - random-direction line searches.
4. Return the best valid sequence found before the time limit.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def clip_valid(a, limit=1000.0):
    """Return a flat float array with all invalid entries replaced by 0."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    return np.clip(arr, 0.0, limit)


def score_array(a):
    """Fast internal evaluator equivalent to evaluate_sequence for valid input."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
    if b.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def half_binomial(n):
    """
    Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated to length n.
    1, 1/2, 3/8, 5/16, 35/128, ...
    """
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    v = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    v[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        v[i + 1] = v[i] * (2.0 * i + 1.0) / (2.0 * i + 2.0)
    return v


def two_block(n, t=0.5):
    """Two-block stepped candidate: first half 1, second half t."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    v = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    half = n // 2
    v[half:] = float(t)
    return v


def normalize_peak(a):
    """Scale so max(convolve(a,a)) is 1, when possible."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
    if b.size == 0:
        return arr
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if np.isfinite(m) and m > 0.0:
        return arr / np.sqrt(m)
    return arr


def build_candidates(seed):
    """Generate a diverse set of initial candidates."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands = []

    lengths = [16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192]
    for n in lengths:
        cands.append(half_binomial(n))
        cands.append(two_block(n, 0.5))

    # Two-block ratios on moderate lengths.
    for n in [128, 256, 512]:
        for t in (0.35, 0.45, 0.5, 0.6, 0.75):
            cands.append(two_block(n, t))

    # Modulated/power variants of the half-binomial template.
    for n in [64, 128, 256, 512]:
        h = half_binomial(n)
        if n <= 1:
            continue
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        for r in (0.9, 0.95, 1.05, 1.1):
            cands.append(np.power(h, r))
        for al in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.4, 0.8):
            for be in (-1.5, -0.75, 0.75, 1.5):
                cands.append(h * np.exp(al * x + be * x * x))

    # Random positive starts.
    for n in [64, 128, 256, 512]:
        for _ in range(3):
            v = rng.exponential(scale=1.0, size=n)
            v = np.maximum(v, 1e-6)
            cands.append(v)

    # Optional use of the provided previous construction.
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    if h is not None:
        arr = clip_valid(h)
        if arr.size > 0:
            if arr.size <= 8192:
                cands.append(arr)
            else:
                m = 4096
                k = arr.size // m
                if k > 0:
                    cands.append(arr[:k * m].reshape(m, k).mean(axis=1))
                else:
                    cands.append(np.ones(256, dtype=float))

    return cands


def evaluate_top(cands, limit):
    """Score candidates and return the best 'limit' as (score, array)."""
    ref = []
    scored = []
    idx = 0
    for a in cands:
        arr = clip_valid(a)
        if arr.size == 0 or arr.size > 16384:
            continue
        sc = score_array(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), idx))
        ref.append(arr)
        idx += 1

    scored.sort()
    top = []
    for sc, i in scored[:limit]:
        top.append((sc, ref[i]))
    return top


def line_search_improve(a, d, current_score):
    """
    Search along a + t*d for t in a small set.
    Returns (candidate, score) if strictly better, else (None, inf).
    """
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    dirr = np.asarray(d, dtype=float)
    if arr.shape != dirr.shape or arr.size == 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(dirr)):
        return None, float("inf")
    if not np.any(dirr):
        return None, float("inf")

    tmax = 2.0
    neg = dirr < 0.0
    if np.any(neg):
        ratios = -arr[neg] / dirr[neg]
        tmax = float(np.min(ratios)) * (1.0 - 1e-10)
        if tmax > 1.0:
            tmax = 1.0

    if not np.isfinite(tmax) or tmax <= 1e-12:
        return None, float("inf")

    best_sc = current_score
    best_cand = None
    ts = [tmax, 0.75 * tmax, 0.5 * tmax, 0.25 * tmax, 0.125 * tmax, 0.0625 * tmax]
    for t in ts:
        if not np.isfinite(t) or t <= 0.0 or t > tmax:
            continue
        cand = arr + t * dirr
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(cand))
        if s < 0.01:
            continue
        sc = score_array(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-11:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_cand = cand
    return best_cand, best_sc


def stateoftart_direction(f):
    """
    Solve the convex LP from the described state-of-the-art iteration:
        maximize sum(g)
        subject to f*g <= 1, g >= 0.
    Assumes max(convolve(f,f)) is 1.
    Returns g if it gives larger sum than f, else None.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=float)
    n = f.size
    if n <= 0 or n > 512:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(f)) or np.sum(f) <= 0.0:
        return None

    # Constraint matrix: (f*g)[k] = sum_j f[k-j] g[j].
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=float)
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        l = max(0, k - n + 1)
        r = min(n - 1, k)
        if l <= r:
            A[k, l:r + 1] = f[k - r:k - l + 1][::-1]

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    b = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)

    U = max(1.0, 2.0 * float(np.sum(f)))
    bounds = []
    for _ in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, float(U)))

    try:
        res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is not None and res.success:
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(g)) and float(np.sum(g)) > float(np.sum(f)) + 1e-8:
            return np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return None


def stateoftart_improve(a, start_score, time_limit):
    """Iteratively apply the LP state-of-the-art direction and line-search."""
    arr = normalize_peak(np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy())
    sc = score_array(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = start_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    for _ in range(35):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        g = stateoftart_direction(arr)
        if g is None:
            break
        d = g - arr
        cand, sc2 = line_search_improve(arr, d, sc)
        if cand is None:
            break
        arr = cand
        sc = float(sc2)
        arr = normalize_peak(arr)
        sc0 = score_array(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc0):
            sc = sc0
    return arr, float(sc)


def build_active(a, tol=1e-5, max_rows=600):
    """
    Build gradients of active convolution-coefficients.
    The constraints are g_k = (a*a)[k] <= 1 after peak normalization.
    """
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = arr.size
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
    if b.size == 0:
        return None
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return None
    b = b / m
    active = np.where(b >= 1.0 - tol)[0]
    if active.size == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    elif active.size > max_rows:
        order = np.argsort(-b[active])
        active = active[order[:max_rows]]

    G = np.zeros((active.size, n), dtype=float)
    for idx, k in enumerate(active):
        l = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
        r = min(n - 1, int(k))
        if l <= r:
            # d(a*a)[k] / da[i] = 2*a[k-i] for valid i.
            G[idx, l:r + 1] = 2.0 * arr[k - r:k - l + 1][::-1]
    return G


def solve_active_direction(a, G):
    """
    Linearized direction problem:
        maximize    sum(d) - 0.5*M
        subject to  grad_k(c) . d  <= M   for active k
                    -a_i <= d_i <= max(a_i,1).
    """
    if linprog is None or G is None or G.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    m = G.shape[0]

    cvec = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    cvec[:n] = -1.0
    cvec[n] = 0.5

    A_ub = np.zeros((m, n + 1), dtype=float)
    A_ub[:, :n] = G
    A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
    b_ub = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)

    lows = -a
    ups = np.maximum(a, 1.0)

    bounds = []
    for i in range(n):
        bounds.append((float(lows[i]), float(ups[i])))
    bounds.append((None, None))

    try:
        res = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is not None and res.success:
        obj = float(cvec @ res.x)
        if obj < -1e-7:
            return np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
    return None


def active_improve(a, start_score, time_limit):
    """Local search using active-constraint linearized directions."""
    arr = normalize_peak(np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy())
    sc = score_array(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = start_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    no_improve = 0
    for _ in range(60):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline or no_improve > 2:
            break
        arr = normalize_peak(arr)
        sc = score_array(arr)

        G = build_active(arr, tol=1e-5, max_rows=600)
        d = solve_active_direction(arr, G)
        if d is None:
            G = build_active(arr, tol=1e-3, max_rows=600)
            d = solve_active_direction(arr, G)
        if d is None:
            break

        cand, sc2 = line_search_improve(arr, d, sc)
        if cand is None:
            no_improve += 1
            break
        arr = cand
        sc = float(sc2)
        no_improve = 0
    return arr, float(sc)


def block_local_search(a, start_score, time_limit, rng):
    """Simple robust block-scaling hill climber."""
    arr = normalize_peak(np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy())
    sc = score_array(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = start_score
    n = arr.size
    if n <= 0:
        return arr, float("inf")

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    if n <= 128:
        max_iters = 6000
    elif n <= 256:
        max_iters = 3000
    elif n <= 1024:
        max_iters = 1000
    elif n <= 4096:
        max_iters = 250
    else:
        max_iters = 60

    minw = max(1, n // 500)
    maxw = max(minw + 1, min(n, max(2, n // 16)))

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iters:
        it += 1
        cand = arr.copy()
        w = int(rng.integers(minw, maxw + 1))
        if w <= 0:
            w = 1
        s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
        e = min(n, s + w)
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 4))

        if mode == 0:
            cand[s:e] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))
        elif mode == 1:
            cand[s:e] *= float(rng.uniform(0.35, 2.0))
        elif mode == 2:
            val = float(np.mean(arr)) * float(rng.uniform(0.4, 2.0))
            cand[s:e] = val
        else:
            s2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
            if s2 <= s:
                s2 = s + w
            e2 = min(n, s2 + w)
            if s2 < n and e2 > s2:
                tmp = cand[s:e].copy()
                cand[s:e] = cand[s2:e2]
                cand[s2:e2] = tmp

        cand = clip_valid(cand)
        if cand.sum() <= 0.0:
            continue
        sc2 = score_array(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-11:
            arr = cand
            sc = float(sc2)
    return arr, float(sc)


def random_direction_search(a, start_score, time_limit, rng):
    """Random direction hill-climbing with line search."""
    arr = normalize_peak(np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy())
    sc = score_array(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = start_score
    n = arr.size
    if n <= 0:
        return arr, float("inf")

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    if n <= 128:
        max_iters = 3000
    elif n <= 256:
        max_iters = 1500
    elif n <= 512:
        max_iters = 600
    else:
        max_iters = 120

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        d = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            d += 0.05
        lows = -0.5 * arr
        highs = np.maximum(0.5 * arr, 0.5)
        d = np.clip(d, lows, highs)
        if np.sum(d) <= 0.0:
            d = -d
            d = np.clip(d, lows, highs)
        cand, sc2 = line_search_improve(arr, d, sc)
        if cand is not None:
            arr = cand
            sc = float(sc2)
    return arr, float(sc)


def modulation_search(base_a, base_score, rng, deadline):
    """Try random parameterized deformations of the half-binomial template."""
    best_a = np.asarray(base_a, dtype=float).copy()
    best_sc = float(base_score)
    choices = [64, 128, 256, 512]
    count = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and count < 800:
        count += 1
        n = int(rng.choice(choices))
        h = half_binomial(n)
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        r = float(rng.uniform(0.85, 1.15))
        al = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7))
        be = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7))
        v = np.power(h, r) * np.exp(al * x + be * x * x)
        v = clip_valid(v)
        sc = score_array(v)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc + 0.02:
            v2, sc2 = block_local_search(v, sc, 2.0, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_a = v2
                best_sc = float(sc2)
                print("modulation improvement:", best_sc)
    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Main entry point: returns one concrete nonnegative sequence."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    cands = build_candidates(seed)
    top = evaluate_top(cands, 12)

    if not top:
        fallback = half_binomial(256)
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    best_a, best_sc = top[0]
    print("initial best score:", best_sc, "length:", best_a.size)

    # LP/polish phases on the best moderate-length candidates.
    for idx in range(min(4, len(top))):
        sc, a = top[idx]
        if sc > best_sc + 0.5:
            continue
        if a.size > 384:
            continue
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 60.0:
            break
        limit1 = min(45.0, rem / 3.0)
        limit2 = min(30.0, max(10.0, rem / 3.0))
        if limit1 >= 5.0:
            a2, sc2 = stateoftart_improve(a, sc, limit1)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_a, best_sc = a2, sc2
                print("state-of-art LP improvement:", best_sc, "length:", best_a.size)
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 30.0:
            a2, sc2 = active_improve(a2, sc2, limit2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_a, best_sc = a2, sc2
                print("active-constraint LP improvement:", best_sc, "length:", best_a.size)

    # Block hill-climb the current global best.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0:
        limit = min(120.0, rem * 0.35)
        a2, sc2 = block_local_search(best_a, best_sc, limit, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a, best_sc = a2, sc2
            print("block hill-climb improvement:", best_sc, "length:", best_a.size)

    # Random direction hill-climb if the best is not too long.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0 and best_a.size <= 512:
        limit = min(90.0, rem * 0.30)
        a2, sc2 = random_direction_search(best_a, best_sc, limit, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a, best_sc = a2, sc2
            print("random direction improvement:", best_sc, "length:", best_a.size)

    # Modulated half-binomial exploration.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        a2, sc2 = modulation_search(best_a, best_sc, rng, deadline - 60.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a, best_sc = a2, sc2
            print("modulation search improvement:", best_sc, "length:", best_a.size)

    # Short block polish on a few other promising top candidates.
    for idx in range(1, len(top)):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 60.0:
            break
        sc, a = top[idx]
        if a.size <= 2048 and sc < best_sc + 0.25:
            a2, sc2 = block_local_search(a, sc, 30.0, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_a, best_sc = a2, sc2
                print("second candidate block improvement:", best_sc, "length:", best_a.size)

    # Final cleanup and official validation.
    best_a = clip_valid(best_a)
    if best_a.size > 0:
        s = float(np.sum(best_a))
        if s > 0.0:
            best_a = best_a * (np.sqrt(2.0 * best_a.size) / s)
        best_a = clip_valid(best_a)

    official = float("inf")
    try:
        official = float(evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        official = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(official):
        best_a = half_binomial(512)
        official = float("inf")
        try:
            official = float(evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            official = 2.0

    print("final length:", best_a.size, "final score:", official)
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
